# tools-depth

## capability_config_journey
CL 1
L tools-depth v1 -- Capability catalog changes alter real search and plans
X A changed catalog file or digest is treated as proof even though search and planning still use different behavior -> Run the bound capability config journey; require live search/describe/plan output and a persisted plan to reflect the changed field.
F Selected capability plans are not evidence that adapter work ran; execution remains owned by a separate contract.
M tools-depth "In disposable state, the journey changes a real capability description, observes search/describe/planner results and plan persistence, then removes the term and reloads the catalog. A second case configures an unregistered adapter and requires an unavailable description and adapter_required plan without executing it." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Run the registered capability-config-journey adapter in isolated scratch state. The contract covers local discovery and planning only; it does not prove external adapter execution, rendered UI, provider work or publication." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## proofs-c-runtime
CL 1
L tools-depth v1 -- PROOFS-c runtime contracts
X A successful response or existing receipt alone does not prove a managed action. -> Run the proofs-c-runtime startup adapter in a fresh isolated root; it verifies bytes, persistence, real subprocesses and refusal paths.
F OCI authenticated evidence, trust removal, tampering, activation/rollback and interrupted-transaction recovery: retain all8 OCI cases until full real verifier journeys are migrated.
F Upstream GitHub availability is not claimed: declared metadata is served on owned loopback48487, then production parser and persisted cache run; no package downloads.
M tools-depth "runtime.memory.durable: Saving and adding memory persist exact content, tags, kind, unique identity and session/objective provenance in order; reopening preserves the same items." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "runtime.memory.search: Search returns positive lexical overlaps ranked by overlap and tag count; empty or absent matches fall back to recent entries and honor the limit." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "runtime.memory.ingest: Ingestion adds last3 decisions, last2 risks and first2 next actions with exact kinds/tags/session/objective; preserve previous items and return inserted ids in order." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "runtime.mcp.discovery: Server/search observers omit schemas; describe preserves qualified identity, cached schema and mutability. Codex command-index maps to stdio; SSE remains unavailable with an explicit frontier." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "runtime.mcp.authorization: Unauthenticated and unapproved mutating actions cannot dispatch transport calls; success requires dispatch, authenticated state, approval when required and no invalid schema result." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "runtime.mcp.receipt: Every broker action persists its exact returned schema/provider/tool/auth/result/mission receipt under the selected root, including refusal receipts." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "runtime.mcp.progressive: Register exactly mcp.servers/search/describe/call; progressive calls use the broker authorization/receipt path. Explicit StubTransport handlers remain simulation." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "runtime.mcp.host: Neyvia MCP JSON-RPC host lists progressive tools without schemas, forwards qualified calls into the broker and marks approval refusals as MCP errors." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## proofs-e-release
CL 1
L tools-depth v1 -- Release and capability observations (PROOFS-e)
X Metadata readiness or a scoped proof is mistaken for external execution or a published release -> Inspect the observed boundary and use the owning real activation or publication procedure
F No installer build, installation, publication, download or private signing-key provisioning is claimed.
F Enabled plugin metadata and native instruction linkage do not prove external MCP/plugin execution.
F The impact index proves static command wiring; it does not execute backend or desktop commands.
M tools-depth "Imported command aliases resolve only the named source registry in the selected repository" src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Backend handler evidence requires actual equality or membership branches, including resolved aliases and unions" src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "UI command evidence comes from recognized gateways, command slots and typed command registries; display strings and comments stay excluded" src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "The impact graph keeps handled commands, branch locations and UI caller provenance consistent" src:"authored manual" state:verified

## proofs-e-sv
CL 1
L tools-depth v1 -- PROOFS-e s-v host contracts and confined startup receipts
X A passed scratch validator or receipt reducer is mistaken for provider, native release, NAS or rendered UI proof -> Read the adapter boundary and retain every unmapped case in the migration frontier
F config/proofs/proofs-e-sv.json lists exact retained cases and the missing host/journey receipt; pending cases are never deletion authority.
M tools-depth "Production calls enforce these semantic contracts before returning. Startup entry: grant_agent.proofs_e_sv.self_check with a fresh confined root." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Run the registered proofs-e-sv area through neyvia.verify to obtain real scratch process, file, archive, language-compiler and local HTTP receipts." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "This chapter is separate from other area additions; sv.* contract IDs identify its host-owned guarantees." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## proofs_a_capabilities
CL 1
L tools-depth v1 -- Capability action contracts (PROOFS-a)
X Scratch receipt is mistaken for external runtime or rendered UI evidence -> Use a real rendered or authorized external journey before claiming those boundaries
F Unmapped original cases remain protected until complete equivalent contracts and startup receipts exist
M tools-depth "Behavior plans preserve selected capsule gates and bounded learned vectors; compiler cache remains observable." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Challenge selection follows configured token relevance; checkpoint saving verifies the durable full record." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Capability descriptions bind actual adapter state; artifact lineage binds local file hashes and persistent relation endpoints." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Typed recovery remains review-only and does not retain narrative file bodies." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Run neyvia verify --areas a-capabilities in isolated scratch state." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "App Factory static verification admits an inactive local draft; native compilation and rendered notes behavior require separate receipts." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "External application surfaces accept only hash-bound signed host authority; approval planning never launches a process." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Startup authority documents are lab fixtures; their readiness status is not proof of a real listening server." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Capability initialization uses an explicit empty vault configuration under disposable proof state. Saved broker/account configurations are never read; these procedures make no vault/account execution claim." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## proofs_a_capability_catalog
CL 1
L tools-depth v1 -- Capability action contracts (PROOFS-a)
X Scratch receipt is mistaken for external runtime or rendered UI evidence -> Use a real rendered or authorized external journey before claiming those boundaries
F Unmapped original cases remain protected until complete equivalent contracts and startup receipts exist
M tools-depth "Behavior plans preserve selected capsule gates and bounded learned vectors; compiler cache remains observable." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Challenge selection follows configured token relevance; checkpoint saving verifies the durable full record." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Capability descriptions bind actual adapter state; artifact lineage binds local file hashes and persistent relation endpoints." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Typed recovery remains review-only and does not retain narrative file bodies." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Run neyvia verify --areas a-capabilities in isolated scratch state." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Capability initialization uses an explicit empty vault configuration under disposable proof state. Saved broker/account configurations are never read; these procedures make no vault/account execution claim." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## proofs_a_capability_execution
CL 1
L tools-depth v1 -- PROOFS-a capability plans, execution admission and durable previews
X Discovery alone never proves an execution handler. Agent delegation remains an explicit handoff. -> Require the admitted executable handler or report the explicit delegation handoff.
F No live external runtime or hardware proof is claimed by the local catalog workflow.
M tools-depth "Every compiled plan binds its canonical content and final verification stage. Persisted preview phases survive service restart. Approved custom packs reload from scoped storage. Startup uses real local files, production backend dispatch and MCP calls with no eager schemas." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Capability initialization uses an explicit empty vault configuration under disposable proof state. Saved broker/account configurations are never read; these procedures make no vault/account execution claim." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## proofs_a_connected_observation
CL 1
L tools-depth v1 -- PROOFS-a connected app observation and scoped session state
X Status and policy observations do not execute transfers or grant new authority. Generated local receipts do not activate or publish an app. -> Inspect policy and authority receipts; separately authorize any transfer.
F No NAS, real cloud credential, external provider or native desktop build proof is claimed.
M tools-depth "Startup observes a finite read-only HTTP bridge on owned port48462 and closes it. Installed Chromium operates actual production-generated drafts: add, search, complete, reload, and export notes; then reject incomplete/proof-free guided runs, seal a reviewed workflow receipt, independently hash its exported bytes, reload and explicitly return outcomes." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Capability initialization uses an explicit empty vault configuration under disposable proof state. Saved broker/account configurations are never read; these procedures make no vault/account execution claim." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## proofs_a_typed_tools_and_replay
CL 1
L tools-depth v1 -- PROOFS-a typed tools, authored argv and honest browser replay
X Declared contract readiness is distinct from actual executable/version/hash readiness. Replay metrics cannot prove a live change. No unapproved device command may start. -> Validate actual version, hash and authority before starting an executable command.
F Pandoc, LibreOffice, Wasmtime, Syft/Grype and Defender real execution journeys remain unproven because their configured managed installations are absent. Two planned game capability declarations lack catalog ownership.
M tools-depth "Load validated pinned metadata and Word reference bytes. Startup runs actual local Python argv and installed Poppler two-page extraction, checks typed private compatibility projections, rejects unapproved Android/outside-workspace calls before invoking a fixture executor, records approval/heartbeat/disconnect metadata, and executes replay on a separate scratch worker with no NAS fallback. Reviewed renewed guided apps preserve exact inactive lineage through copy/replay/rebind refusal and actual Chromium exports." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Capability initialization uses an explicit empty vault configuration under disposable proof state. Saved broker/account configurations are never read; these procedures make no vault/account execution claim." src:"authored manual" state:verified

## tools
CL 1
L tools-depth v1 -- Hash-bound edits and cited cached research
T t1{sha256:str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$" content:str ..}
T t2{document:str ~"^doc_[a-f0-9]{64}$" ..}
T t3 str ~"^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$"
T t4 json:"{\"type\":\"object\"}"
T t5 [{start:0.. end:0.. text:str expectedText?:str}]#1..100
T t6 str ~"^doc_[a-f0-9]{64}$"
T t7 [str ~"^doc_[a-f0-9]{64}$"]#1..100
T t8{groups:json:"{\"type\":\"array\"}" inputCount:int uniqueCount:int method:"exact-observed-text-sha256" ..}
S tools-depth.file:t1=workspace.read(path:path)
S tools-depth.document:t2=web.read(document:document maxChars:512)
A workspace.read(path:str maxChars?:1..100000 offset?:0.. startLine?:1.. endLine?:1.. expectedSha256?:t3) -> t1 -- Read bounded character or inclusive line ranges with the original-byte hash; optional expectedSha256 refuses mixed snapshots
F verify-workspace-read "No authored observer check is bound to workspace.read" -> ask operator blocks:workspace.read
A workspace.search(query:str includeGlob?:str maxResults?:1..200) -> t4 -- Search confined eligible text with ripgrep, or reported Python fallback; incomplete results stay marked incomplete
F verify-workspace-search "No authored observer check is bound to workspace.search" -> ask operator blocks:workspace.search
A workspace.patch(path:str#1.. expectedSha256:t3 edits:t5) -> t4 ! -- Apply all ranges against one hash-checked snapshot; serialize cooperative writers, atomically persist and verify bytes
C workspace.patch persisted-text:matches(workspace.read(maxChars:100000 path:path) {properties:{content:{const:expectedContent} truncated:{const:false}} required:["content" "truncated"] type:"object"})
A web.fetch(url:str maxChars?:200..100000 refresh?:bool maxAgeSeconds?:0..86400) -> t2 ! -- Fetch at most 2 MiB and write or refresh a workspace cache entry with an immutable document handle; reuse a recent URL observation unless refresh=true
C web.fetch cached-document:web.read(document:result.document maxChars:512) .document == result.document
A web.read(document:t6 offset?:0.. maxChars?:1..100000) -> t2 -- Continue cached text by character offset without a network call
F verify-web-read "No authored observer check is bound to web.read" -> ask operator blocks:web.read
A web.passages(document:t6 query:str#1..1000 offset?:0.. limit?:1..50 contextChars?:0..1000) -> t4 -- Search literal text, returning bounded quote ranges, source URLs, hashes and continuation
F verify-web-passages "No authored observer check is bound to web.passages" -> ask operator blocks:web.passages
A web.cite(document:t6 start:0.. end:1.. expectedText?:str#..4000) -> t4 -- Return an exact observed source quote; expectedText mismatch fails
F verify-web-cite "No authored observer check is bound to web.cite" -> ask operator blocks:web.cite
A web.dedupe(documents:t7) -> t8 -- Group identical observed text while keeping every URL and content hash; no semantic deduplication
F verify-web-dedupe "No authored observer check is bound to web.dedupe" -> ask operator blocks:web.dedupe
C workspace.read persisted-text:matches(workspace.read(maxChars:100000 path:path) {properties:{content:{const:expectedContent} truncated:{const:false}} required:["content" "truncated"] type:"object"})
C web.read cached-document:web.read(document:fetched.document maxChars:512) .document == fetched.document
P guarded-edit(path:str#1.. edits:t5 expectedContent:str#..100000):observed=workspace.read(path:path); J review-edit=apply; patched=workspace.patch(edits:edits expectedSha256:observed.sha256 path:path) C persisted-text -- Observe a file, approve the supplied snapshot ranges, patch and verify fresh persisted text (up to 100000 characters)
V P guarded-edit -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
P cited-research(url:str query:str#1..1000):fetched=web.fetch(maxChars:512 url:url) C cached-document; passages=web.passages(document:fetched.document limit:3 query:query); J source-relevance -- Fetch a source, verify its cached identity and find quote-backed passages
V P cited-research -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
P verify-effect-workspace-patch(path:str#1.. expectedSha256:t3 edits:t5):effect=workspace.patch(edits:edits expectedSha256:expectedSha256 path:path) -- Verify the actual effect through fresh owning observers
V P verify-effect-workspace-patch -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
P fetch-owned-http-body(maxAgeSeconds:0..86400 maxChars:200..100000 refresh:bool url:str):observed=web.fetch(maxAgeSeconds:maxAgeSeconds maxChars:maxChars refresh:refresh url:url) -- Fetch the exact HTTP response and verify retained original bytes and parsed document
V P fetch-owned-http-body -> script why:"typed manual runner; stops at every judgement"
J review-edit apply|leave:"Do the proposed character ranges match the observed file and intended edit?" -- Ranges refer to Unicode characters in this exact snapshot. A hash conflict requires a fresh read; judge decisions grant no additional permission.
V J review-edit -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
J source-relevance use|search-more:"Do these exact passages support the answer?" -- Use the returned URL and quote ranges; partial responseTruncated sources are not complete documents. Cite only a nonempty returned range.
V J source-relevance -> human:operator why:"explicit choice required"
X File changed or range overlaps -> Read the new snapshot and recompute ranges; never reuse the old hash
X Search incomplete or fallback reported -> Narrow includeGlob and inspect engine, skipped and fallbackReason
X Source changed after refresh -> Old handles retain their old quotes; use the new handle for current claims
F Arbitrary external file writers cannot participate in the cooperative edit lock
F HTTP text extraction does not execute JavaScript or extract PDF bytes
F MCP verification proves one validated call, not every tool or external-provider certification
M tools-depth "Coordinates count Unicode characters, including original CRLF; workspace.read returns absolute offset for selected line ranges." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "MCP readiness comes from existing mcp.servers/search/describe/call; configured, connected, discovered, verified, failing. Simulation never verifies." src:"authored manual" state:verified
M tools-depth "Existing model.tools.feedback and model.tools.feedback.record use incremental SQLite aggregates; recorded success is evidence of a tool call, not task completion." src:"authored manual" state:verified
